{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_016EPbZpCYz3C9iEN2ESf57w","content":"Launching skill: frontend-playbook"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"9cd5bc7d-a4c0-431e-96d5-b62c79e3123a","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:46:15.434Z","tool_use_result":{"success":true,"commandName":"frontend-playbook"}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\n## What You Probably Got Wrong\n\n**\"I'll use `yarn chain`.\"** Wrong. `yarn chain` gives you an empty local chain with no protocols, no tokens, no state. `yarn fork --network base` gives you a copy of real Base with Uniswap, Aave, USDC, real whale balances — everything (verified addresses: `addresses/SKILL.md`). Always fork.\n\n**\"I deployed to IPFS and it works.\"** Did the CID change? If not, you deployed stale output. Did routes work? Without `trailingSlash: true`, every route except `/` returns 404. Did you check the OG image? Without `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL`, it points to `localhost:3000`.\n\n**\"I'll set up the project manually.\"** Don't. `npx create-eth@latest` handles everything — Foundry, Next.js, RainbowKit, scaffold hooks. Never run `forge init` or create Next.js projects from scratch.\n\n---\n\n## Fork Mode Setup\n\n### Why Fork, Not Chain\n\n```\nyarn chain (WRONG)              yarn fork --network base (CORRECT)\n└─ Empty local chain            └─ Fork of real Base mainnet\n└─ No protocols                 └─ Uniswap, Aave, etc. available\n└─ No tokens                    └─ Real USDC, WETH exist\n└─ Testing in isolation         └─ Test against REAL state\n```\n\n### Setup\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@latest          # Select: foundry, target chain, name\ncd <project-name>\nyarn install\nyarn fork --network base       # Terminal 1: fork of real Base\nyarn deploy                    # Terminal 2: deploy contracts to fork\nyarn start                     # Terminal 3: Next.js frontend\n```\n\n### Critical: Chain ID Gotcha\n\n**When using fork mode, the frontend target network MUST be `chains.foundry` (chain ID 31337), NOT the chain you're forking.**\n\nThe fork runs locally on Anvil with chain ID 31337. Even if you're forking Base:\n\n```typescript\n// scaffold.config.ts during development\ntargetNetworks: [chains.foundry],  // ✅ NOT chains.base!\n```\n\nOnly switch to `chains.base` when deploying contracts to the REAL network.\n\n### Enable Block Mining\n\n```bash\n# In a new terminal — REQUIRED for time-dependent logic\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nWithout this, `block.timestamp` stays FROZEN. Any contract logic using timestamps (deadlines, expiry, vesting) will break silently.\n\n**Make it permanent** by editing `packages/foundry/package.json` to add `--block-time 1` to the fork script.\n\n---\n\n## Deploying to IPFS (Recommended)\n\nIPFS is the recommended deploy path for SE2. Avoids Vercel's memory limits entirely. Produces a fully decentralized static site.\n\n### Full Build Command\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out  # ALWAYS clean first\n\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://yourapp.yourname.eth.link\" \\\n  NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true \\\n  yarn build\n\n# Upload to BuidlGuidl IPFS\nyarn bgipfs upload out\n# Save the CID!\n```\n\n### Node 25+ localStorage Polyfill (REQUIRED)\n\nNode.js 25+ ships a built-in `localStorage` object that's MISSING standard WebStorage API methods (`getItem`, `setItem`). This breaks `next-themes`, RainbowKit, and any library that calls `localStorage.getItem()` during static page generation.\n\n**Error you'll see:**\n```\nTypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function\nError occurred prerendering page \"/_not-found\"\n```\n\n**The fix:** Create `polyfill-localstorage.cjs` in `packages/nextjs/`:\n```javascript\nif (typeof globalThis.localStorage !== \"undefined\" &&\n    typeof globalThis.localStorage.getItem !== \"function\") {\n  const store = new Map();\n  globalThis.localStorage = {\n    getItem: (key) => store.get(key) ?? null,\n    setItem: (key, value) => store.set(key, String(value)),\n    removeItem: (key) => store.delete(key),\n    clear: () => store.clear(),\n    key: (index) => [...store.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    get length() { return store.size; },\n  };\n}\n```\n\n**Why `--require` and not `instrumentation.ts`?** Next.js spawns a separate build worker process for prerendering. `--require` injects into EVERY Node process (including workers). `next.config.ts` polyfill only runs in the main process. `instrumentation.ts` doesn't run in the build worker. Only `--require` works.\n\n### IPFS Routing — Why Routes Break\n\nIPFS gateways serve static files. No server handles routing. Three things MUST be true:\n\n**1. `output: \"export\"` in next.config.ts** — generates static HTML files.\n\n**2. `trailingSlash: true` (CRITICAL)** — This is the #1 reason routes break:\n- `trailingSlash: false` (default) → generates `debug.html`\n- `trailingSlash: true` → generates `debug/index.html`\n- IPFS gateways resolve directories to `index.html` automatically, but NOT bare filenames\n- Without trailing slash: `/debug` → 404 ❌\n- With trailing slash: `/debug` → `debug/` → `debug/index.html` ✅\n\n**3. Pages must survive static prerendering** — any page that crashes during `yarn build` (browser APIs at import time, localStorage) gets skipped silently → 404 on IPFS.\n\n**The complete IPFS-safe next.config.ts pattern:**\n```typescript\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\n**SE2's block explorer pages** use `localStorage` at import time and crash during static export. Rename `app/blockexplorer` to `app/_blockexplorer-disabled` if not needed.\n\n### Stale Build Detection\n\n**The #1 IPFS footgun:** You edit code, then deploy the OLD build.\n\n```bash\n# MANDATORY after ANY code change:\nrm -rf .next out                     # 1. Delete old artifacts\n# ... run full build command ...     # 2. Rebuild from scratch\ngrep -l \"YOUR_STRING\" out/_next/static/chunks/app/*.js  # 3. Verify changes present\n\n# Timestamp check:\nstat -f '%Sm' app/page.tsx           # Source modified time\nstat -f '%Sm' out/                   # Build output time\n# Source NEWER than out/ = STALE BUILD. Rebuild first!\n```\n\n**The CID is proof:** If the IPFS CID didn't change after a deploy, you deployed the same content. A real code change ALWAYS produces a new CID.\n\n### Verify Routes After Deploy\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html                  # Each route has a directory + index.html\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://GATEWAY/ipfs/CID/debug/\"\n# Should return 200, not 404\n```\n\n---\n\n## Deploying to Vercel (Alternative)\n\nSE2 is a monorepo — Vercel needs special configuration.\n\n### Configuration\n\n1. **Root Directory:** `packages/nextjs`\n2. **Install Command:** `cd ../.. && yarn install`\n3. **Build Command:** leave default (`next build`)\n4. **Output Directory:** leave default (`.next`)\n\n```bash\n# Via API:\ncurl -X PATCH \"https://api.vercel.com/v9/projects/PROJECT_ID\" \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $VERCEL_TOKEN\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"rootDirectory\": \"packages/nextjs\", \"installCommand\": \"cd ../.. && yarn install\"}'\n```\n\n### Common Failures\n\n| Error | Cause | Fix |\n|-------|-------|-----|\n| \"No Next.js version detected\" | Root Directory not set | Set to `packages/nextjs` |\n| \"cd packages/nextjs: No such file\" | Build command has `cd` | Clear it — root dir handles this |\n| OOM / exit code 129 | SE2 monorepo exceeds 8GB | Use IPFS instead, or `vercel --prebuilt` |\n\n### Decision Tree\n\n```\nWant to deploy SE2?\n├─ IPFS (recommended) → yarn ipfs / manual build + upload\n│   └─ Fully decentralized, no memory limits, works with ENS\n├─ Vercel → Set rootDirectory + installCommand\n│   └─ Fast CDN, but centralized. May OOM on large projects\n└─ vercel --prebuilt → Build locally, push artifacts to Vercel\n    └─ Best of both: local build power + Vercel CDN\n```\n\n---\n\n## ENS Subdomain Setup\n\nTwo mainnet transactions to point an ENS subdomain at your IPFS deployment.\n\n### Transaction 1: Create Subdomain (new apps only)\n\n1. Open `https://app.ens.domains/yourname.eth`\n2. Go to \"Subnames\" tab → \"New subname\"\n3. Enter the label (e.g. `myapp`) → Next → Skip profile → Open Wallet → Confirm\n4. If gas is stuck: switch MetaMask to Ethereum → Activity tab → \"Speed up\"\n\n### Transaction 2: Set IPFS Content Hash\n\n1. Navigate to `https://app.ens.domains/myapp.yourname.eth`\n2. \"Records\" tab → \"Edit Records\" → \"Other\" tab\n3. Paste in Content Hash field: `ipfs://<CID>`\n4. Save → Open Wallet → Confirm in MetaMask\n\nFor **updates** to an existing app: skip Tx 1, only do Tx 2.\n\n### Verify\n\n```bash\n# 1. Onchain content hash matches\nRESOLVER=$(cast call 0x00000000000C2e074eC69A0dFb2997BA6C7d2e1e \\\n  \"resolver(bytes32)(address)\" $(cast namehash myapp.yourname.eth) \\\n  --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com)\ncast call $RESOLVER \"contenthash(bytes32)(bytes)\" \\\n  $(cast namehash myapp.yourname.eth) --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com\n\n# 2. Gateway responds (may take 5-15 min for cache)\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\"\n\n# 3. OG metadata correct (not localhost)\ncurl -s -L \"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\" | grep 'og:image'\n```\n\n**Use `.eth.link` NOT `.eth.limo`** — `.eth.link` works better on mobile.\n\n---\n\n## Go to Production — Complete Checklist\n\nWhen the user says \"ship it\", follow this EXACT sequence.\n\n### Step 1: Final Code Review 🤖\n- All feedback incorporated\n- No duplicate h1, no raw addresses, no shared isLoading\n- `scaffold.config.ts` has `rpcOverrides` and `pollingInterval: 3000`\n\n### Step 2: Choose Domain 👤\nAsk: *\"What subdomain do you want? e.g. `myapp.yourname.eth` → `myapp.yourname.eth.link`\"*\n\n### Step 3: Generate OG Image + Fix Metadata 🤖\n- Create 1200×630 PNG (`public/thumbnail.png`) — NOT the stock SE2 thumbnail\n- Set `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL` to the live domain\n- Verify `og:image` will resolve to an absolute production URL\n\n### Step 4: Clean Build + IPFS Deploy 🤖\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs && rm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\" \\\n  NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true \\\n  yarn build\n\n# Verify before uploading:\nls out/*/index.html                        # Routes exist\ngrep 'og:image' out/index.html             # Not localhost\nstat -f '%Sm' app/page.tsx                 # Source older than out/\nstat -f '%Sm' out/\n\nyarn bgipfs upload out                     # Save the CID\n```\n\n### Step 5: Share for Approval 👤\nSend: *\"Build ready for review: `https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>`\"*\n**Wait for approval before touching ENS.**\n\n### Step 6: Set ENS 🤖\nCreate subdomain (if new) + set IPFS content hash. Two mainnet transactions.\n\n### Step 7: Verify 🤖\n- Content hash matches onchain\n- `.eth.link` gateway responds with 200\n- OG image loads correctly\n- Routes work (`/debug/`, etc.)\n\n### Step 8: Report 👤\n*\"Live at `https://myapp.yourname.eth.link` — ENS content hash confirmed onchain, unfurl metadata set.\"*\n\n---\n\n## Build Verification Process\n\nA build is NOT done when the code compiles. It's done when you've tested it like a real user.\n\n### Phase 1: Code QA (Automated)\n- Scan `.tsx` files for raw address strings (should use `<Address/>`)\n- Scan for shared `isLoading` state across multiple buttons\n- Scan for missing `disabled` props on transaction buttons\n- Verify RPC config and polling interval\n- Verify OG metadata with absolute URLs\n- Verify no public RPCs in any file\n\n### Phase 2: Smart Contract Testing\n```bash\nforge test                    # All tests pass\nforge test --fuzz-runs 10000  # Fuzz testing\n```\nTest edge cases: zero amounts, max amounts, unauthorized callers, reentrancy attempts.\n\n### Phase 3: Browser Testing (THE REAL TEST)\n\nOpen the app and do a FULL walkthrough:\n\n1. **Load the app** — does it render correctly?\n2. **Check page title** — is it correct, not \"Scaffold-ETH 2\"?\n3. **Connect wallet** — does the connect flow work?\n4. **Wrong network** — connect on wrong chain, verify \"Switch to Base\" appears\n5. **Switch network** — click the switch button, verify it works\n6. **Approve flow** — verify approve button shows, click it, wait for tx, verify action button appears\n7. **Main action** — click primary action, verify loader, wait for tx, verify state updates\n8. **Error handling** — reject a transaction in wallet, verify UI recovers\n9. **Address displays** — all addresses showing ENS/blockies, not raw hex?\n10. **Share URL** — check OG unfurl (image, title, description)\n\n### Phase 4: QA Sub-Agent (Complex Builds)\nFor bigger projects, spawn a sub-agent with fresh context. Give it the repo path and deployed URL. It reads all code against the UX rules, opens a browser, clicks through independently, and reports issues.\n\n---\n\n## Don't Do These\n\n- ❌ `yarn chain` — use `yarn fork --network <chain>`\n- ❌ `forge init` — use `npx create-eth@latest`\n- ❌ Manual Next.js setup — SE2 handles it\n- ❌ Manual wallet connection — SE2 has RainbowKit pre-configured\n- ❌ Edit `deployedContracts.ts` — it's auto-generated by `yarn deploy`\n- ❌ Hardcode API keys in `scaffold.config.ts` — use `.env.local`\n- ❌ Use `mainnet.base.org` in production — use Alchemy or similar\n\n---\n\n## Resources\n\n- **SE2 Docs:** https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/\n- **UI Components:** https://ui.scaffoldeth.io/\n- **SpeedRun Ethereum:** https://speedrunethereum.com/\n- **ETH Tech Tree:** https://www.ethtechtree.com\n- **BuidlGuidl IPFS:** https://upload.bgipfs.com\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"adef0486-37c2-403d-83e2-ea527b7ca4cc","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:46:15.434Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FgAoB14bAPHWjEdp51cdbR","type":"tool_result","content":"===== foundry/contracts/YourContract.sol =====\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n    // State Variables\n    address public immutable owner;\n    string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n    bool public premium = false;\n    uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n    // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n    event GreetingChange(address indexed greetingSetter, string newGreeting, bool premium, uint256 value);\n\n    // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n    // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n    constructor(address _owner) {\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n    // Check the withdraw() function\n    modifier isOwner() {\n        // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n        require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n     *\n     * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n     */\n    function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n        // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n        console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n        console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n        greeting = _newGreeting;\n        totalCounter += 1;\n        userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n        // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n        if (msg.value > 0) {\n            premium = true;\n        } else {\n            premium = false;\n        }\n\n        // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n        emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n     * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n     */\n    function withdraw() public isOwner {\n        (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n        require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n     */\n    receive() external payable { }\n}\n===== script/DeployYourContract.s.sol =====\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AqwQD54E3w4n2WKXRtPadm","type":"tool_result","content":"===== Makefile fork/chain =====\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n===== foundry.toml =====\n[profile.default]\nsrc = 'contracts'\nout = 'out'\nlibs = ['lib', 'node_modules']\nfs_permissions = [{ access = \"read-write\", path = \"./\"}]\nextra_output = [\"evm.bytecode.opcodes\"]\noptimizer = true\noptimizer_runs = 200\n\n\n[rpc_endpoints]\ndefault_network = \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\"\n\nmainnet = \"https://eth-mainnet.alchemyapi.io/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\nsepolia = \"https://eth-sepolia.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\narbitrum = \"https://arb-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\narbitrumSepolia = \"https://arb-sepolia.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\noptimism = \"https://opt-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\noptimismSepolia = \"https://opt-sepolia.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\npolygon = \"https://polygon-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\npolygonMumbai = \"https://polygon-mumbai.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\npolygonZkEvm = \"https://zkevm-rpc.com\"\npolygonZkEvmTestnet = \"https://rpc.public.zkevm-test.net\"\ngnosis = \"https://rpc.gnosischain.com\"\nchiado = \"https://rpc.chiadochain.net\"\nbase = \"https://mainnet.base.org\"\nbaseSepolia = \"https://sepolia.base.org\"\nscrollSepolia = \"https://sepolia-rpc.scroll.io\"\nscroll = \"https://rpc.scroll.io\"\npgn = \"https://rpc.publicgoods.network\"\npgnTestnet = \"https://sepolia.publicgoods.network\"\n\n\nlocalhost = \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\"\n\n[etherscan]\npolygonMumbai = { key = \"${ETHERSCAN_API_KEY}\" }\nsepolia = { key = \"${ETHERSCAN_API_KEY}\" }\n\n\n\n[fmt]\nline_length = 120\ntab_width = 4\nquote_style = \"double\"\nbracket_spacing = true\nint_types = \"long\"\n\n\n\n\n# See more config options https://book.getfoundry.sh/reference/config/overview===== .env.example =====\n# Template for foundry environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# We provide default values so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY is used while deploying contract.\n# On anvil chain the value of it can be empty since we use the prefunded account\n# which comes with anvil chain to deploy contract.\n# NOTE: You don't need to manually change the value of DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY, it should\n# be auto filled when run `yarn generate`.\n\n# Alchemy rpc URL is used while deploying the contracts to some testnets/mainnets, checkout `foundry.toml` for it's use.\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\n# Etherscan API key is used to verify the contract on etherscan.\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=DNXJA8RX2Q3VZ4URQIWP7Z68CJXQZSC6AW\n# Default account for localhost / use \"scaffold-eth-custom\" if you wish to use a generated account or imported account\nLOCALHOST_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT=scaffold-eth-default","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"cd0cb48b-e383-4cde-b8a2-fb0dc52983f5","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:48:34.131Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"===== {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMcvSS1ZG5PK58TUbU99","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PUZhavqb98sUAUM61qmxZE","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @title TipJar\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n * @notice A public tip jar that accepts USDC tips (with an optional message) and\n *         keeps an onchain feed of every tip. The jar owner can withdraw the\n *         accumulated USDC at any time.\n *\n * @dev USDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is a two-step flow for the tipper:\n *      1. `approve(tipJar, amount)` on the USDC token\n *      2. `tip(amount, message)` on this contract, which pulls the USDC via\n *         `transferFrom`.\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts (Base USDC: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913).\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice The address allowed to withdraw the collected tips.\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    /// @notice Longest allowed tip message, in bytes.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from;\n        uint256 amount; // in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals)\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, oldest first.\n    Tip[] private tips;\n\n    /// @notice Gross amount of USDC ever tipped (does not decrease on withdraw).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Total amount tipped per address.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @param _usdc  Address of the USDC token contract to accept.\n     * @param _owner Address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n     */\n    constructor(IERC20 _usdc, address _owner) {\n        usdc = _usdc;\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @dev Requires the caller to have `approve`d this contract for at least `amount`.\n     * @param amount  Amount of USDC (6 decimals) to tip.\n     * @param message Optional message to attach to the tip (max 280 bytes).\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        // Pull the USDC from the tipper. SafeERC20 reverts on failure.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        tips.push(Tip({ from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Withdraw the entire USDC balance of the jar to the owner.\n     */\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, balance);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar.\n    function jarBalance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips in the feed.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return the whole tip feed, oldest first.\n    function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        return tips;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Return the `count` most recent tips, newest first.\n     * @dev Handy for a UI that only wants to show a recent slice of the feed.\n     */\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        Tip[] memory recent = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            recent[i] = tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return recent;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":341,"cache_read_input_tokens":49487,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":341},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"2117dc17-f560-4fce-80c1-ee2450dc6c8e","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:49:47.813Z","request_id":"req_011CdMcvQqWzzy91D5QdTdTR"}

The jar owner can withdraw the\n *         accumulated USDC at any time.\n *\n * @dev USDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is a two-step flow for the tipper:\n *      1. `approve(tipJar, amount)` on the USDC token\n *      2. `tip(amount, message)` on this contract, which pulls the USDC via\n *         `transferFrom`.\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts (Base USDC: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913).\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice The address allowed to withdraw the collected tips.\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    /// @notice Longest allowed tip message, in bytes.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from;\n        uint256 amount; // in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals)\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, oldest first.\n    Tip[] private tips;\n\n    /// @notice Gross amount of USDC ever tipped (does not decrease on withdraw).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Total amount tipped per address.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @param _usdc  Address of the USDC token contract to accept.\n     * @param _owner Address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n     */\n    constructor(IERC20 _usdc, address _owner) {\n        usdc = _usdc;\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @dev Requires the caller to have `approve`d this contract for at least `amount`.\n     * @param amount  Amount of USDC (6 decimals) to tip.\n     * @param message Optional message to attach to the tip (max 280 bytes).\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        // Pull the USDC from the tipper. SafeERC20 reverts on failure.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        tips.push(Tip({ from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Withdraw the entire USDC balance of the jar to the owner.\n     */\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, balance);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar.\n    function jarBalance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips in the feed.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return the whole tip feed, oldest first.\n    function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        return tips;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Return the `count` most recent tips, newest first.\n     * @dev Handy for a UI that only wants to show a recent slice of the feed.\n     */\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        Tip[] memory recent = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            recent[i] = tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return recent;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMcxK4K6TRMDQmWNPqVK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01ViqQBWa3FMtvU3dr7eNYNf","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for the TipJar contract.\n * @dev Deploys a jar that accepts Base USDC. When running against a fork of Base\n *      (see `yarn fork`), the real USDC contract already lives at this address,\n *      so approve/transferFrom work against real token state.\n *\n * The USDC address can be overridden with the USDC_ADDRESS env var if needed.\n */\ncontract DeployTipJar is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    // Base mainnet USDC.\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        address usdc = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", BASE_USDC);\n        new TipJar(IERC20(usdc), deployer);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMd1AaALmP8X4bPMqDkY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TqGdE88HYcdEMkoYRzEk46","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: UNLICENSED\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n\n/// @dev Minimal 6-decimal token that mimics USDC for unit tests.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") { }\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC internal usdc;\n    TipJar internal jar;\n\n    address internal owner = address(0xABCD);\n    address internal alice = address(0xA11CE);\n    address internal bob = address(0xB0B);\n\n    uint256 internal constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(IERC20(address(usdc)), owner);\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testConfig() public view {\n        assertEq(address(jar.usdc()), address(usdc));\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), owner);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function testTipMovesUsdcAndRecordsFeed() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory tips = jar.getTips();\n        assertEq(tips.length, 1);\n        assertEq(tips[0].from, alice);\n        assertEq(tips[0].amount, 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(tips[0].message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testEmitsNewTip() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), ONE_USDC);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, false, false, true);\n        emit TipJar.NewTip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"thanks\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testMultipleTipsAndRecentOrder() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"third\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 3);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 6 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 4 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        // getRecentTips returns newest first\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n\n        // asking for more than exists clamps to total\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory all = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(all.length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function testRevertOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testRevertOnLongMessage() public {\n        string memory long = new string(281);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), ONE_USDC);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.MessageTooLong.selector);\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, long);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testRevertWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // SafeERC20 reverts when allowance is insufficient\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approve\");\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawOnlyOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"for you\");\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        uint256 ownerBefore = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), ownerBefore + 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 0);\n        // totalTipped is a lifetime counter and does not decrease\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function testFuzzTip(uint256 amount, address tipper) public {\n        vm.assume(tipper != address(0) && tipper != address(jar));\n        amount = bound(amount, 1, 1_000_000 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        usdc.mint(tipper, amount);\n        vm.startPrank(tipper);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, \"fuzz\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), amount);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(tipper), amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":229,"cache_read_input_tokens":59635,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":229},"output_tokens":59,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"74c386db-21b8-4c74-ba5d-b0e4e8a23d58","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:50:53.438Z","request_id":"req_011CdMd19HXPxWsseafY5fvT"}

@dev Minimal 6-decimal token that mimics USDC for unit tests.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") { }\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC internal usdc;\n    TipJar internal jar;\n\n    address internal owner = address(0xABCD);\n    address internal alice = address(0xA11CE);\n    address internal bob = address(0xB0B);\n\n    uint256 internal constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(IERC20(address(usdc)), owner);\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testConfig() public view {\n        assertEq(address(jar.usdc()), address(usdc));\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), owner);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function testTipMovesUsdcAndRecordsFeed() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory tips = jar.getTips();\n        assertEq(tips.length, 1);\n        assertEq(tips[0].from, alice);\n        assertEq(tips[0].amount, 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(tips[0].message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testEmitsNewTip() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), ONE_USDC);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, false, false, true);\n        emit TipJar.NewTip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"thanks\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testMultipleTipsAndRecentOrder() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"third\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 3);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 6 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 4 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        // getRecentTips returns newest first\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n\n        // asking for more than exists clamps to total\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory all = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(all.length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function testRevertOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testRevertOnLongMessage() public {\n        string memory long = new string(281);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), ONE_USDC);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.MessageTooLong.selector);\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, long);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testRevertWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // SafeERC20 reverts when allowance is insufficient\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approve\");\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawOnlyOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"for you\");\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        uint256 ownerBefore = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), ownerBefore + 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 0);\n        // totalTipped is a lifetime counter and does not decrease\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function testFuzzTip(uint256 amount, address tipper) public {\n        vm.assume(tipper != address(0) && tipper != address(jar));\n        amount = bound(amount, 1, 1_000_000 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        usdc.mint(tipper, amount);\n        vm.startPrank(tipper);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"126bb5ea-2d63-4a08-81b2-3609c4c3f281","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:51:34.103Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/Makefile","oldString":"# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337","newString":"# Start a fork\n# --block-time 1 keeps block.timestamp advancing so time-dependent logic and\n# the tip feed's timestamps behave like a live chain.\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337 --block-time 1","originalFile":".PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n# Generate TypeScript ABIs\ngenerate-abis:\n\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n\n# List account\naccount:\n\t@node scripts-js/checkAccountBalance.js\n\n# Get address of a keystore\nget-address:\n\t@cast wallet address --account $(ACCOUNT_NAME)\n\n# Compile contracts\ncompile:\n\tforge compile\n\n# Flatten contracts\nflatten:\n\tforge flatten\n\n# Format code\nformat:\n\tforge fmt && prettier --write ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Lint code\nlint:\n\tforge fmt --check && prettier --check ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Run tests\ntest:\n\tforge test\n\n# Verify contracts\nverify:\n\tforge script script/VerifyAll.s.sol --ffi --rpc-url $(RPC_URL)\n\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":12,"oldLines":8,"newStart":12,"newLines":10,"lines":["   anvil"," "," # Start a fork","+# --block-time 1 keeps block.timestamp advancing so time-dependent logic and","+# the tip feed's timestamps behave like a live chain."," fork: setup-anvil-wallet","-  anvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337","+  anvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337 --block-time 1"," "," # Deploy the contracts"," deploy:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMdDmt2ZD4qwA8zrBL8e","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HtrcKSnkwzVqCsTRUJkg8y","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/scripts-js/fundUsdc.js","content":"#!/usr/bin/env node\n/**\n * Fund an address with USDC on the local Base fork.\n *\n * Real USDC can't be minted normally, but on a fork we can impersonate USDC's\n * masterMinter, authorize a minter, and mint. This gives any address spendable\n * USDC so you can try the tip flow end to end.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   yarn fund-usdc <address> [amount]\n *   # amount is in whole USDC (default 1000)\n *   yarn fund-usdc 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 500\n */\nimport { execSync } from \"child_process\";\n\nconst RPC = process.env.RPC_URL || \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\";\nconst USDC = \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\";\n\nconst recipient = process.argv[2];\nconst wholeAmount = process.argv[3] ? Number(process.argv[3]) : 1000;\n\nif (!recipient || !/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/.test(recipient)) {\n  console.error(\"Usage: yarn fund-usdc <address> [amountInWholeUsdc]\");\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst amount = BigInt(Math.round(wholeAmount * 1e6)); // USDC has 6 decimals\nconst oneEth = \"0xde0b6b3a7640000\"; // 1 ETH for gas\n\nconst sh = cmd => execSync(cmd, { stdio: [\"ignore\", \"pipe\", \"pipe\"] }).toString().trim();\n\ntry {\n  // Sanity check the fork is reachable\n  sh(`cast chain-id --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n} catch {\n  console.error(`Could not reach a chain at ${RPC}. Is \\`yarn fork\\` running?`);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\ntry {\n  const masterMinter = sh(`cast call ${USDC} \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n\n  // Impersonate masterMinter and authorize the recipient as a minter.\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount ${masterMinter} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_setBalance ${masterMinter} ${oneEth} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(\n    `cast send ${USDC} \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" ${recipient} ${amount} --from ${masterMinter} --unlocked --rpc-url ${RPC}`,\n  );\n\n  // Impersonate the recipient and mint USDC to itself.\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_setBalance ${recipient} ${oneEth} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount ${recipient} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(`cast send ${USDC} \"mint(address,uint256)\" ${recipient} ${amount} --from ${recipient} --unlocked --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n\n  // Stop impersonating so the accounts behave normally again.\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount ${masterMinter} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount ${recipient} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n\n  const bal = sh(`cast call ${USDC} \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" ${recipient} --rpc-url ${RPC}`).split(\" \")[0];\n  console.log(`Minted ${wholeAmount} USDC to ${recipient}`);\n  console.log(`New USDC balance: ${Number(bal) / 1e6} USDC`);\n} catch (err) {\n  console.error(\"Failed to fund USDC:\", err.stderr?.toString() || err.message);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2139,"cache_read_input_tokens":75392,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2139},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"ef5c409b-3460-4ea5-9ccf-5ab8fa1ff094","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:53:40.347Z","request_id":"req_011CdMdDkaeB9gGz1VqcUSJX"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HtrcKSnkwzVqCsTRUJkg8y","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/scripts-js/fundUsdc.js (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"b0612c04-b25c-4772-86a5-8209f7fdb1b5","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:53:40.354Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/scripts-js/fundUsdc.js","content":"#!/usr/bin/env node\n/**\n * Fund an address with USDC on the local Base fork.\n *\n * Real USDC can't be minted normally, but on a fork we can impersonate USDC's\n * masterMinter, authorize a minter, and mint. This gives any address spendable\n * USDC so you can try the tip flow end to end.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   yarn fund-usdc <address> [amount]\n *   # amount is in whole USDC (default 1000)\n *   yarn fund-usdc 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 500\n */\nimport { execSync } from \"child_process\";\n\nconst RPC = process.env.RPC_URL || \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\";\nconst USDC = \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\";\n\nconst recipient = process.argv[2];\nconst wholeAmount = process.argv[3] ? Number(process.argv[3]) : 1000;\n\nif (!recipient || !/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/.test(recipient)) {\n  console.error(\"Usage: yarn fund-usdc <address> [amountInWholeUsdc]\");\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst amount = BigInt(Math.round(wholeAmount * 1e6)); // USDC has 6 decimals\nconst oneEth = \"0xde0b6b3a7640000\"; // 1 ETH for gas\n\nconst sh = cmd => execSync(cmd, { stdio: [\"ignore\", \"pipe\", \"pipe\"] }).toString().trim();\n\ntry {\n  // Sanity check the fork is reachable\n  sh(`cast chain-id --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n} catch {\n  console.error(`Could not reach a chain at ${RPC}. Is \\`yarn fork\\` running?`);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\ntry {\n  const masterMinter = sh(`cast call ${USDC} \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n\n  // Impersonate masterMinter and authorize the recipient as a minter.\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount ${masterMinter} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_setBalance ${masterMinter} ${oneEth} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(\n    `cast send ${USDC} \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" ${recipient} ${amount} --from ${masterMinter} --unlocked --rpc-url ${RPC}`,\n  );\n\n  // Impersonate the recipient and mint USDC to itself.\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_setBalance ${recipient} ${oneEth} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount ${recipient} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(`cast send ${USDC} \"mint(address,uint256)\" ${recipient} ${amount} --from ${recipient} --unlocked --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n\n  // Stop impersonating so the accounts behave normally again.\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount ${masterMinter} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n  sh(`cast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount ${recipient} --rpc-url ${RPC}`);\n\n  const bal = sh(`cast call ${USDC} \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" ${recipient} --rpc-url ${RPC}`).split(\" \")[0];\n  console.log(`Minted ${wholeAmount} USDC to ${recipient}`);\n  console.log(`New USDC balance: ${Number(bal) / 1e6} USDC`);\n} catch (err) {\n  console.error(\"Failed to fund USDC:\", err.stderr?.toString() || err.message);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMdMkEmzQib3wSAqcktY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GJ8vYoLN6BLtmRf2MMJWrm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\nconst MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\nconst PRESETS = [\"1\", \"5\", \"10\", \"25\"];\n\n/**\n * Form to send a USDC tip. USDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is two steps:\n * approve the TipJar to spend USDC, then call `tip`. The button adapts to\n * whichever step is needed next.\n */\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: tipJarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const tipJarAddress = tipJarInfo?.address;\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, tipJarAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isMining: isTipping } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  let amountWei: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    amountWei = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    amountWei = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const hasValidAmount = amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = hasValidAmount && usdcBalance !== undefined && amountWei > usdcBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && (allowance === undefined || allowance < amountWei);\n  const messageTooLong = message.length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!amountWei || !tipJarAddress) return;\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJarAddress, amountWei] });\n    } catch (e) {\n      // errors are surfaced by the scaffold write hook's notification\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!amountWei) return;\n    try {\n      await writeTipJar({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [amountWei, message] });\n      notification.success(\"Tip sent! Thank you 🙏\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const balanceLabel =\n    usdcBalance !== undefined ? `${Number(formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString()} USDC` : \"—\";\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title m-0\">Send a tip</h2>\n          <span className=\"text-sm opacity-70\">Balance: {balanceLabel}</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n          <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Amount (USDC)</label>\n          <div className=\"join\">\n            <input\n              className=\"input input-bordered join-item w-full\"\n              inputMode=\"decimal\"\n              placeholder=\"0.00\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, \"\"))}\n            />\n            <span className=\"btn btn-ghost join-item no-animation pointer-events-none\">USDC</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2 mt-1\">\n            {PRESETS.map(p => (\n              <button key={p} type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-xs btn-outline\" onClick={() => setAmount(p)}>\n                ${p}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n          <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Message (optional)</label>\n          <textarea\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n            placeholder=\"Say something nice…\"\n            rows={2}\n            maxLength={MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <span className={`text-xs self-end ${messageTooLong ? \"text-error\" : \"opacity-60\"}`}>\n            {message.length}/{MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <div className=\"alert text-sm\">Connect your wallet to send a tip.</div>\n        ) : insufficientBalance ? (\n          <div className=\"alert alert-warning text-sm\">\n            Not enough USDC. On the local fork, run{\" \"}\n            <code className=\"whitespace-nowrap\">yarn fund-usdc &lt;your-address&gt;</code> to get some.\n          </div>\n        ) : null}\n\n        {needsApproval ? (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            disabled={!isConnected || !hasValidAmount || insufficientBalance || isApproving}\n            onClick={handleApprove}\n          >\n            {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : null}\n            Approve USDC\n          </button>\n        ) : (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            disabled={!isConnected || !hasValidAmount || insufficientBalance || messageTooLong || isTipping}\n            onClick={handleTip}\n          >\n            {isTipping ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : null}\n            Send tip\n          </button>\n        )}\n\n        {hasValidAmount && needsApproval && !insufficientBalance ? (\n          <p className=\"text-xs opacity-60 -mt-2\">\n            Step 1 of 2: approve the tip jar to move your USDC, then the button becomes “Send tip”.\n          </p>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2546,"cache_read_input_tokens":87079,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2546},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"64a88245-3f89-4109-9784-5cae0ab29482","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:55:54.962Z","request_id":"req_011CdMdMicJ7HMfbBzUq2PS5"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GJ8vYoLN6BLtmRf2MMJWrm","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"eb581f8c-07f5-4216-82ea-aa67d346efbe","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:55:54.973Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\nconst MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\nconst PRESETS = [\"1\", \"5\", \"10\", \"25\"];\n\n/**\n * Form to send a USDC tip. USDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is two steps:\n * approve the TipJar to spend USDC, then call `tip`. The button adapts to\n * whichever step is needed next.\n */\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: tipJarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const tipJarAddress = tipJarInfo?.address;\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, tipJarAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isMining: isTipping } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  let amountWei: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    amountWei = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    amountWei = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const hasValidAmount = amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = hasValidAmount && usdcBalance !== undefined && amountWei > usdcBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && (allowance === undefined || allowance < amountWei);\n  const messageTooLong = message.length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!amountWei || !tipJarAddress) return;\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJarAddress, amountWei] });\n    } catch (e) {\n      // errors are surfaced by the scaffold write hook's notification\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!amountWei) return;\n    try {\n      await writeTipJar({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [amountWei, message] });\n      notification.success(\"Tip sent! Thank you 🙏\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const balanceLabel =\n    usdcBalance !== undefined ? `${Number(formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString()} USDC` : \"—\";\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title m-0\">Send a tip</h2>\n          <span className=\"text-sm opacity-70\">Balance: {balanceLabel}</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n          <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Amount (USDC)</label>\n          <div className=\"join\">\n            <input\n              className=\"input input-bordered join-item w-full\"\n              inputMode=\"decimal\"\n              placeholder=\"0.00\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, \"\"))}\n            />\n            <span className=\"btn btn-ghost join-item no-animation pointer-events-none\">USDC</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2 mt-1\">\n            {PRESETS.map(p => (\n              <button key={p} type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-xs btn-outline\" onClick={() => setAmount(p)}>\n                ${p}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n          <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Message (optional)</label>\n          <textarea\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n            placeholder=\"Say something nice…\"\n            rows={2}\n            maxLength={MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <span className={`text-xs self-end ${messageTooLong ? \"text-error\" : \"opacity-60\"}`}>\n            {message.length}/{MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <div className=\"alert text-sm\">Connect your wallet to send a tip.</div>\n        ) : insufficientBalance ? (\n          <div className=\"alert alert-warning text-sm\">\n            Not enough USDC. On the local fork, run{\" \"}\n            <code className=\"whitespace-nowrap\">yarn fund-usdc &lt;your-address&gt;</code> to get some.\n          </div>\n        ) : null}\n\n        {needsApproval ? (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            disabled={!isConnected || !hasValidAmount || insufficientBalance || isApproving}\n            onClick={handleApprove}\n          >\n            {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : null}\n            Approve USDC\n          </button>\n        ) : (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            disabled={!isConnected || !hasValidAmount || insufficientBalance || messageTooLong || isTipping}\n            onClick={handleTip}\n          >\n            {isTipping ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : null}\n            Send tip\n          </button>\n        )}\n\n        {hasValidAmount && needsApproval && !insufficientBalance ? (\n          <p className=\"text-xs opacity-60 -mt-2\">\n            Step 1 of 2: approve the tip jar to move your USDC, then the button becomes “Send tip”.\n          </p>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMdR6DtzC3CxYNQ4hD9i","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01A6U5N8nthbn8XZUw3ixCaK","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipFeed.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nconst formatWhen = (timestamp: bigint) => {\n  const date = new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000);\n  const diffSec = Math.floor((Date.now() - date.getTime()) / 1000);\n  if (diffSec < 60) return \"just now\";\n  if (diffSec < 3600) return `${Math.floor(diffSec / 60)}m ago`;\n  if (diffSec < 86400) return `${Math.floor(diffSec / 3600)}h ago`;\n  return date.toLocaleDateString();\n};\n\n/**\n * Live feed of tips read straight from the TipJar contract. `getTips` returns\n * the whole feed oldest-first; we reverse it to show the newest tip on top.\n * The scaffold read hook re-fetches on every new block, so new tips appear\n * automatically.\n */\nexport const TipFeed = () => {\n  const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    functionName: \"getTips\",\n  });\n\n  const ordered = tips ? [...tips].reverse() : [];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title m-0\">Tip feed</h2>\n          <span className=\"badge badge-ghost\">{ordered.length}</span>\n        </div>\n\n        {isLoading && ordered.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"flex justify-center py-8\">\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner\" />\n          </div>\n        ) : ordered.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"opacity-60 text-sm py-6 text-center\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop one in the jar!</p>\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-3 max-h-[28rem] overflow-y-auto pr-1\">\n            {ordered.map((tip, i) => (\n              <li key={ordered.length - i} className=\"border border-base-300 rounded-box p-3 flex flex-col gap-1\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n                  <Address address={tip.from} size=\"sm\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n                  <span className=\"font-bold text-primary whitespace-nowrap\">\n                    {Number(formatUnits(tip.amount, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString()} USDC\n                  </span>\n                </div>\n                {tip.message ? <p className=\"text-sm break-words m-0\">{tip.message}</p> : null}\n                <span className=\"text-xs opacity-50\">{formatWhen(tip.timestamp)}</span>\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4347,"cache_read_input_tokens":89625,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4347},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"235e260e-06ab-44c3-a3d8-8d55d695c128","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:56:08.763Z","request_id":"req_011CdMdR4pK4UR2ezevGpL7X"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMdSyUPrkSPfvxkqqFH9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VkSuW5LMQg4VPZXjsXY91N","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { TipFeed } from \"~~/components/tipjar/TipFeed\";\nimport { TipForm } from \"~~/components/tipjar/TipForm\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nconst usdc = (value: bigint | undefined) =>\n  value !== undefined ? `${Number(formatUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString()} USDC` : \"—\";\n\nconst Home: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n\n  const { data: tipJarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const { data: totalTipped } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"totalTipped\" });\n  const { data: jarBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"jarBalance\" });\n  const { data: tipCount } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"tipCount\" });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col grow items-center pt-8 px-4\">\n      <div className=\"text-center max-w-2xl\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-4xl font-bold mb-2\">USDC Tip Jar</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-lg opacity-80 m-0\">\n          Drop a USDC tip on Base and leave a message. Every tip is recorded onchain and shows up in the live feed\n          below.\n        </p>\n        <div className=\"flex justify-center items-center gap-2 mt-4 text-sm\">\n          <span className=\"opacity-70\">Connected:</span>\n          {connectedAddress ? (\n            <Address address={connectedAddress} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"sm\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n          ) : (\n            <span className=\"opacity-50\">not connected</span>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal shadow bg-base-100 mt-8\">\n        <div className=\"stat place-items-center\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Total tipped</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-primary text-2xl\">{usdc(totalTipped)}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat place-items-center\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">In the jar</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{usdc(jarBalance)}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat place-items-center\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Tips</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{tipCount !== undefined ? tipCount.toString() : \"—\"}</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {tipJarInfo?.address ? (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mt-3 text-xs opacity-70\">\n          <span>Jar contract:</span>\n          <Address address={tipJarInfo.address} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"xs\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-6 mt-8 w-full max-w-4xl justify-center pb-12\">\n        <TipForm />\n        <TipFeed />\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default Home;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":818,"cache_read_input_tokens":95839,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":818},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"bf734de4-b050-4bae-8d37-ae5b0090e0ff","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:56:36.055Z","request_id":"req_011CdMdSxKSZF6fBbSCYM9p3"}
