(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"29d09c03-6bf5-4fa9-9e00-fff094ebec6a","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:57:00.660Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx","oldString":"  const hasValidAmount = amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = hasValidAmount && usdcBalance !== undefined && amountWei > usdcBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && (allowance === undefined || allowance < amountWei);","newString":"  const hasValidAmount = amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance =\n    amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n && usdcBalance !== undefined && amountWei > usdcBalance;\n  const needsApproval =\n    amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n && (allowance === undefined || allowance < amountWei);","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\nconst MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\nconst PRESETS = [\"1\", \"5\", \"10\", \"25\"];\n\n/**\n * Form to send a USDC tip. USDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is two steps:\n * approve the TipJar to spend USDC, then call `tip`. The button adapts to\n * whichever step is needed next.\n */\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: tipJarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const tipJarAddress = tipJarInfo?.address;\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, tipJarAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isMining: isTipping } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  let amountWei: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    amountWei = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    amountWei = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const hasValidAmount = amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = hasValidAmount && usdcBalance !== undefined && amountWei > usdcBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && (allowance === undefined || allowance < amountWei);\n  const messageTooLong = message.length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!amountWei || !tipJarAddress) return;\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJarAddress, amountWei] });\n    } catch (e) {\n      // errors are surfaced by the scaffold write hook's notification\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!amountWei) return;\n    try {\n      await writeTipJar({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [amountWei, message] });\n      notification.success(\"Tip sent! Thank you 🙏\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const balanceLabel =\n    usdcBalance !== undefined ? `${Number(formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString()} USDC` : \"—\";\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title m-0\">Send a tip</h2>\n          <span className=\"text-sm opacity-70\">Balance: {balanceLabel}</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n          <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Amount (USDC)</label>\n          <div className=\"join\">\n            <input\n              className=\"input input-bordered join-item w-full\"\n              inputMode=\"decimal\"\n              placeholder=\"0.00\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, \"\"))}\n            />\n            <span className=\"btn btn-ghost join-item no-animation pointer-events-none\">USDC</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2 mt-1\">\n            {PRESETS.map(p => (\n              <button key={p} type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-xs btn-outline\" onClick={() => setAmount(p)}>\n                ${p}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n          <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Message (optional)</label>\n          <textarea\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n            placeholder=\"Say something nice…\"\n            rows={2}\n            maxLength={MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <span className={`text-xs self-end ${messageTooLong ? \"text-error\" : \"opacity-60\"}`}>\n            {message.length}/{MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <div className=\"alert text-sm\">Connect your wallet to send a tip.</div>\n        ) : insufficientBalance ? (\n          <div className=\"alert alert-warning text-sm\">\n            Not enough USDC. On the local fork, run{\" \"}\n            <code className=\"whitespace-nowrap\">yarn fund-usdc &lt;your-address&gt;</code> to get some.\n          </div>\n        ) : null}\n\n        {needsApproval ? (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            disabled={!isConnected || !hasValidAmount || insufficientBalance || isApproving}\n            onClick={handleApprove}\n          >\n            {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : null}\n            Approve USDC\n          </button>\n        ) : (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            disabled={!isConnected || !hasValidAmount || insufficientBalance || messageTooLong || isTipping}\n            onClick={handleTip}\n          >\n            {isTipping ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : null}\n            Send tip\n          </button>\n        )}\n\n        {hasValidAmount && needsApproval && !insufficientBalance ? (\n          <p className=\"text-xs opacity-60 -mt-2\">\n            Step 1 of 2: approve the tip jar to move your USDC, then the button becomes “Send tip”.\n          </p>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":45,"oldLines":8,"newStart":45,"newLines":10,"lines":["   }"," ","   const hasValidAmount = amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n;","-  const insufficientBalance = hasValidAmount && usdcBalance !== undefined && amountWei > usdcBalance;","-  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && (allowance === undefined || allowance < amountWei);","+  const insufficientBalance =","+    amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n && usdcBalance !== undefined && amountWei > usdcBalance;","+  const needsApproval =","+    amountWei !== undefined && amountWei > 0n && (allowance === undefined || allowance < amountWei);","   const messageTooLong = message.length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH;"," ","   const handleApprove = async () => {"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011y32aJzFRB48FkFEwwjaHW","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix\n3\tdescription: RainbowKit+wagmi+Next.js dev build fails on @x402 / async-storage optional deps — fix with IgnorePlugin\n4\tmetadata: \n5\t  node_type: memory\n6\t  type: reference\n7\t  originSessionId: d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb\n8\t  modified: 2026-07-24T21:45:17.979Z\n9\t---\n10\t\n11\tRainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) — both `next dev` and `next build` — throw `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (also seen: `@x402/svm/exact/client`, `@x402/evm`, `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). In `next build` these are hard errors that fail the build. Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n12\t\n13\tFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n14\t\n15\t```js\n16\twebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n17\t  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n18\t  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n19\t  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n20\t  return config;\n21\t}\n22\t```\n23\t\n24\tAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n25\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"a5290838-6e5d-4a1e-a9f8-a95420fe00b0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:58:01.118Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix.md","content":"---\nname: rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix\ndescription: RainbowKit+wagmi+Next.js dev build fails on @x402 / async-storage optional deps — fix with IgnorePlugin\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: reference\n  originSessionId: d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb\n  modified: 2026-07-24T21:45:17.979Z\n---\n\nRainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) — both `next dev` and `next build` — throw `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (also seen: `@x402/svm/exact/client`, `@x402/evm`, `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). In `next build` these are hard errors that fail the build. Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n\nFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n\n```js\nwebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n  return config;\n}\n```\n\nAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n","numLines":25,"startLine":1,"totalLines":25}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DbHsUydZ598wHuSjG4wHzx","type":"tool_result","content":"=== dev/start scripts ===\n    \"build\": \"yarn lint && next build\",\n    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n    \"start\": \"next dev\",\n=== next.config ===\nnext.config.ts\nimport type { NextConfig } from \"next\";\n\nconst nextConfig: NextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  devIndicators: false,\n  typescript: {\n    ignoreBuildErrors: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR === \"true\",\n  },\n};\n\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\n\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = {\n    unoptimized: true,\n  };\n}\n\nmodule.exports = nextConfig;","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"5320225a-75b2-4a3a-a55f-7f6a19298f13","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:58:09.450Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"=== dev/start scripts ===\n    \"build\": \"yarn lint && next build\",\n    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n    \"start\": \"next dev\",\n=== next.config ===\nnext.config.ts\nimport type { NextConfig } from \"next\";\n\nconst nextConfig: NextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  devIndicators: false,\n  typescript: {\n    ignoreBuildErrors: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR === \"true\",\n  },\n};\n\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\n\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = {\n    unoptimized: true,\n  };\n}\n\nmodule.exports = nextConfig;","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Ucyc2Un2VmUjg2qbhZ4dc3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n2\t\n3\t<h4 align=\"center\">\n4\t  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n5\t  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n6\t</h4>\n7\t\n8\t🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n9\t\n10\t> [!NOTE]"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"bb1b8cdf-f3b0-4b85-89fd-4b69e4331c7d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:01:37.983Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/README.md","content":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]","numLines":10,"startLine":1,"totalLines":83}}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMdrNc5SCRZ25f17s1mu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XfWXYMR4RC7xkLyScbn1S6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T214559Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/README.md","content":"# 💸 USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA tiny full-stack dApp: an onchain **tip jar** that accepts **USDC tips** on Base,\nplus a web page with a **live tip feed**, a **send-a-tip form**, and a\n**connect-wallet** flow.\n\nEverything runs **locally against a fork of Base mainnet**, so tips move real\nBase USDC (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`) — no testnet faucets and\nnothing deployed to a live network.\n\nBuilt with [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry + Next.js + RainbowKit + wagmi).\n\n---\n\n## What's in here\n\n| Piece | Location |\n| --- | --- |\n| `TipJar` smart contract | `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` |\n| Contract tests (unit + fuzz) | `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` |\n| Deploy script | `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` |\n| USDC-funding helper | `packages/foundry/scripts-js/fundUsdc.js` |\n| Web app (feed + form + wallet) | `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`, `packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/` |\n| USDC token registration for the UI | `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` |\n\n### How the contract works\n\nUSDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is a **two-step** flow for the tipper:\n\n1. `approve(tipJar, amount)` on the USDC token.\n2. `tip(amount, message)` on the `TipJar`, which pulls the USDC via `transferFrom`\n   and appends the tip to an onchain feed.\n\n`TipJar` records every tip (`from`, `amount`, `message`, `timestamp`), exposes the\nfeed via `getTips()` / `getRecentTips(n)`, and lets the **owner** (the deployer)\n`withdraw()` the collected USDC.\n\n---\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **Node.js** — use an LTS that `create-eth` / this toolchain supports:\n  `>= 20.17`, or `>= 22.13` (this project was built and tested on **Node 22.23**).\n  > Note: very new Node (25+) ships a partial global `localStorage` that breaks\n  > Next static prerendering, so stick with Node 22 LTS for a smooth ride.\n  > With `nvm`: `nvm install 22 && nvm use 22`.\n- **Yarn** (v1 classic works; the repo uses Yarn workspaces).\n- **Foundry** (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`) — https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation\n- **git**\n\n---\n\n## Setup & run (local, 3 terminals)\n\nFrom the project root (`usdc-tip-jar/`):\n\n```bash\n# 0. Install dependencies (first time only)\nyarn install\n```\n\n### Terminal 1 — fork Base mainnet\n\n```bash\nyarn fork\n```\n\nThis starts a local Anvil node **forked from Base mainnet** on\n`http://127.0.0.1:8545` with **chain id 31337**. Because it's a fork, the real\nUSDC contract already exists at its Base address, so approvals and transfers work\nagainst real token code. (`--block-time 1` is enabled so `block.timestamp` — and\nyour tip timestamps — keep advancing like a live chain.)\n\n### Terminal 2 — deploy the TipJar\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nDeploys `TipJar` to the fork, pointing it at Base USDC, and writes the address +\nABI into `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` for the frontend.\n\n### Terminal 3 — start the web app\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\n> Uses the webpack bundler (`next dev --webpack`) on purpose — see\n> [Troubleshooting](#troubleshooting).\n\n---\n\n## Sending a tip\n\n1. **Connect a wallet** on the site (top-right button):\n   - The **built-in burner wallet** is the fastest — it's created for you on the\n     local network. Click the connect button and pick it.\n   - Or connect **MetaMask** to a custom network: RPC `http://127.0.0.1:8545`,\n     chain id `31337`. You can import an Anvil dev account, e.g. account #0\n     private key `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`.\n\n2. **Get some test USDC** for your connected address. Real USDC can't be minted\n   normally, but on the fork this helper impersonates USDC's minter and mints to\n   any address:\n\n   ```bash\n   # in a spare terminal, from the project root\n   yarn fund-usdc <your-address>        # defaults to 1000 USDC\n   yarn fund-usdc <your-address> 250    # or a specific amount\n   ```\n\n   (Need ETH for gas too? The burner wallet has a faucet button in the header;\n   imported Anvil accounts already start with plenty of ETH.)\n\n3. On the site, enter an **amount** and an optional **message**, then:\n   - Click **Approve USDC** (step 1 of 2 — lets the jar move your USDC).\n   - Click **Send tip** (step 2 — sends the tip).\n\nYour tip appears in the **Tip feed** immediately, and the stats (total tipped,\nin the jar, tip count) update.\n\n### Withdrawing (owner only)\n\nThe deployer account (Anvil account #9,\n`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) owns the jar and can withdraw:\n\n```bash\ncast send <TIPJAR_ADDRESS> \"withdraw()\" \\\n  --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545\n# TIPJAR_ADDRESS is printed by `yarn deploy` and stored in deployedContracts.ts\n```\n\n---\n\n## Tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test            # runs forge test (unit + fuzz)\n# or, with more fuzzing:\ncd packages/foundry && forge test --fuzz-runs 10000\n```\n\nThe suite covers: config wiring, tip accounting + feed, the `NewTip` event,\nrecent-tips ordering, and reverts for zero amount, over-long messages, missing\napproval, non-owner withdraw, and empty withdraw.\n\n---\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n- **`Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/...'`** — RainbowKit → wagmi pulls in\n  Coinbase/Base account packages that lazily `import()` optional `@x402/*`\n  payment modules this app never installs. The bundler tries to resolve them\n  statically and fails. Fixed in `packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` with a webpack\n  `IgnorePlugin` for `^@x402/`, which is why the dev script runs on webpack\n  (`next dev --webpack`) rather than Turbopack (Turbopack doesn't apply that\n  webpack config).\n- **Frontend shows no data / wrong chain** — make sure Terminal 1 (`yarn fork`)\n  is running and you deployed (Terminal 2). The frontend targets `chains.foundry`\n  (31337) for local development — see `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n- **\"Not enough USDC\"** — run `yarn fund-usdc <your-address>`.\n- **Restarting the fork** wipes chain state (tips, balances). Re-run `yarn deploy`\n  and `yarn fund-usdc` afterward.\n\n---\n\n## Notes\n\n- **Nothing is deployed to a live network.** This is a local project run against a\n  Base fork; the deliverable is the working project and this README.\n- Base USDC address (mainnet and on the fork): `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`.\n- The frontend deliberately targets the local fork (`chains.foundry`, id 31337).\n  To point at real Base later you'd switch `targetNetworks` in\n  `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` and deploy the contract to Base.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":373,"cache_read_input_tokens":123675,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":373},"output_tokens":56,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"1278c413-50c8-4c87-b404-1c7832a565b7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:02:20.670Z","request_id":"req_011CdMdrMCk1UViHApD6WUYJ"}

registration for the UI | `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` |"," ","-## Requirements","+### How the contract works"," ","-Before you begin, you need to install the following tools:","+USDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is a **two-step** flow for the tipper:"," ","-- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)","-- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))","-- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)","+1. `approve(tipJar, amount)` on the USDC token.","+2. `tip(amount, message)` on the `TipJar`, which pulls the USDC via `transferFrom`","+   and appends the tip to an onchain feed."," ","-## Quickstart","+`TipJar` records every tip (`from`, `amount`, `message`, `timestamp`), exposes the","+feed via `getTips()` / `getRecentTips(n)`, and lets the **owner** (the deployer)","+`withdraw()` the collected USDC."," ","-To get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:","+---"," ","-1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:","+## Prerequisites"," ","-```","-cd my-dapp-example","+- **Node.js** — use an LTS that `create-eth` / this toolchain supports:","+  `>= 20.17`, or `>= 22.13` (this project was built and tested on **Node 22.23**).","+  > Note: very new Node (25+) ships a partial global `localStorage` that breaks","+  > Next static prerendering, so stick with Node 22 LTS for a smooth ride.","+  > With `nvm`: `nvm install 22 && nvm use 22`.","+- **Yarn** (v1 classic works; the repo uses Yarn workspaces).","+- **Foundry** (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`) — https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation","+- **git**","+","+---","+","+## Setup & run (local, 3 terminals)","+","+From the project root (`usdc-tip-jar/`):","+","+```bash","+# 0. Install dependencies (first time only)"," yarn install"," ```"," ","-2. Run a local network in the first terminal:","+### Terminal 1 — fork Base mainnet"," ","+```bash","+yarn fork"," ```","-yarn chain","-```"," ","-This command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.","+This starts a local Anvil node **forked from Base mainnet** on","+`http://127.0.0.1:8545` with **chain id 31337**. Because it's a fork, the real","+USDC contract already exists at its Base address, so approvals and transfers work","+against real token code. (`--block-time 1` is enabled so `block.timestamp` — and","+your tip timestamps — keep advancing like a live chain.)"," ","-3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:","+### Terminal 2 — deploy the TipJar"," ","-```","+```bash"," yarn deploy"," ```"," ","-This command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.","+Deploys `TipJar` to the fork, pointing it at Base USDC, and writes the address +","+ABI into `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` for the frontend."," ","-4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:","+### Terminal 3 — start the web app"," ","-```","+```bash"," yarn start"," ```"," ","-Visit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","+Open **http://localhost:3000**."," ","-Run smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`","+> Uses the webpack bundler (`next dev --webpack`) on purpose — see","+> [Troubleshooting](#troubleshooting)."," ","-- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`","-- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.","-- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`","+---"," ","+## Sending a tip"," ","-## Documentation","+1. **Connect a wallet** on the site (top-right button):","+   - The **built-in burner wallet** is the fastest — it's created for you on the","+     local network. Click the connect button and pick it.","+   - Or connect **MetaMask** to a custom network: RPC `http://127.0.0.1:8545`,","+     chain id `31337`. You can import an Anvil dev account, e.g. account #0","+     private key `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`."," ","-Visit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.","+2. **Get some test USDC** for your connected address. Real USDC can't be minted","+   normally, but on the fork this helper impersonates USDC's minter and mints to","+   any address:"," ","-To know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).","+   ```bash","+   # in a spare terminal, from the project root","+   yarn fund-usdc <your-address>        # defaults to 1000 USDC","+   yarn fund-usdc <your-address> 250    # or a specific amount","+   ```"," ","-## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2","+   (Need ETH for gas too? The burner wallet has a faucet button in the header;","+   imported Anvil accounts already start with plenty of ETH.)"," ","-We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!","+3. On the site, enter an **amount** and an optional **message**, then:","+   - Click **Approve USDC** (step 1 of 2 — lets the jar move your USDC).","+   - Click **Send tip** (step 2 — sends the tip)."," ","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file","+Your tip appears in the **Tip feed** immediately, and the stats (total tipped,","+in the jar, tip count) update.","+","+### Withdrawing (owner only)","+","+The deployer account (Anvil account #9,","+`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) owns the jar and can withdraw:","+","+```bash","+cast send <TIPJAR_ADDRESS> \"withdraw()\" \\","+  --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 \\","+  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545","+# TIPJAR_ADDRESS is printed by `yarn deploy` and stored in deployedContracts.ts","+```","+","+---","+","+## Tests","+","+```bash","+yarn foundry:test            # runs forge test (unit + fuzz)","+# or, with more fuzzing:","+cd packages/foundry && forge test --fuzz-runs 10000","+```","+","+The suite covers: config wiring, tip accounting + feed, the `NewTip` event,","+recent-tips ordering, and reverts for zero amount, over-long messages, missing","+approval, non-owner withdraw, and empty withdraw.","+","+---","+","+## Troubleshooting","+","+- **`Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/...'`** — RainbowKit → wagmi pulls in","+  Coinbase/Base account packages that lazily `import()` optional `@x402/*`","+  payment modules this app never installs. The bundler tries to resolve them","+  statically and fails. Fixed in `packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` with a webpack","+  `IgnorePlugin` for `^@x402/`, which is why the dev script runs on webpack","+  (`next dev --webpack`) rather than Turbopack (Turbopack doesn't apply that","+  webpack config).","+- **Frontend shows no data / wrong chain** — make sure Terminal 1 (`yarn fork`)","+  is running and you deployed (Terminal 2). The frontend targets `chains.foundry`","+  (31337) for local development — see `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","+- **\"Not enough USDC\"** — run `yarn fund-usdc <your-address>`.","+- **Restarting the fork** wipes chain state (tips, balances). Re-run `yarn deploy`","+  and `yarn fund-usdc` afterward.","+","+---","+","+## Notes","+","+- **Nothing is deployed to a live network.** This is a local project run against a","+  Base fork; the deliverable is the working project and this README.","+- Base USDC address (mainnet and on the fork): `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`.","+- The frontend deliberately targets the local fork (`chains.foundry`, id 31337).","+  To point at real Base later you'd switch `targetNetworks` in","+  `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` and deploy the contract to Base."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMe2FCry3e8FNcbgxMcE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012knYoJM9pTsnsrePJWJpUE","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix.md","old_string":"Also push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.","new_string":"Also push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n\n**Next.js 16 / Turbopack wrinkle (create-eth 2.0.22 scaffolds Next 16.2, `next dev` defaults to Turbopack):** the webpack IgnorePlugin config above is IGNORED by Turbopack, so `next dev` still 500s with `Can't resolve '@x402/svm/exact/client'`. Fix: force webpack — set the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts to `next dev --webpack` (the `--webpack` flag exists in Next 16). Then the `next.config.ts` webpack IgnorePlugin applies and the page renders. Verified 2026-07-24 building a USDC tip jar on a Base fork."},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":974,"cache_read_input_tokens":134243,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":974},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"d04fd86f-ac2d-4fd7-b4a2-5c7acd7422f1","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:04:04.896Z","request_id":"req_011CdMe2CPhZpUpZK8AWfpS2"}

The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.","newString":"Also push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n\n**Next.js 16 / Turbopack wrinkle (create-eth 2.0.22 scaffolds Next 16.2, `next dev` defaults to Turbopack):** the webpack IgnorePlugin config above is IGNORED by Turbopack, so `next dev` still 500s with `Can't resolve '@x402/svm/exact/client'`. Fix: force webpack — set the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts to `next dev --webpack` (the `--webpack` flag exists in Next 16). Then the `next.config.ts` webpack IgnorePlugin applies and the page renders. Verified 2026-07-24 building a USDC tip jar on a Base fork.","originalFile":"---\nname: rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix\ndescription: RainbowKit+wagmi+Next.js dev build fails on @x402 / async-storage optional deps — fix with IgnorePlugin\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: reference\n  originSessionId: d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb\n  modified: 2026-07-24T21:45:17.979Z\n---\n\nRainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) — both `next dev` and `next build` — throw `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (also seen: `@x402/svm/exact/client`, `@x402/evm`, `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). In `next build` these are hard errors that fail the build. Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n\nFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n\n```js\nwebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n  return config;\n}\n```\n\nAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":5,"oldLines":7,"newStart":5,"newLines":7,"lines":["   node_type: memory","   type: reference","   originSessionId: d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","-  modified: 2026-07-24T21:45:17.979Z","+  modified: 2026-07-24T22:04:04.903Z"," ---"," "," RainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) — both `next dev` and `next build` — throw `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (also seen: `@x402/svm/exact/client`, `@x402/evm`, `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). In `next build` these are hard errors that fail the build. Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it."]},{"oldStart":22,"oldLines":3,"newStart":22,"newLines":5,"lines":[" ```"," "," Also push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.","+","+**Next.js 16 / Turbopack wrinkle (create-eth 2.0.22 scaffolds Next 16.2, `next dev` defaults to Turbopack):** the webpack IgnorePlugin config above is IGNORED by Turbopack, so `next dev` still 500s with `Can't resolve '@x402/svm/exact/client'`. Fix: force webpack — set the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts to `next dev --webpack` (the `--webpack` flag exists in Next 16). Then the `next.config.ts` webpack IgnorePlugin applies and the page renders. Verified 2026-07-24 building a USDC tip jar on a Base fork."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"memdirStamped":true}}

The USDC Tip Jar is built and running locally end-to-end.\n\n## What was built\n\n**Contract** (`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`)\n- Accepts USDC tips via the standard ERC-20 `approve` → `transferFrom` flow, each with an optional message (≤280 bytes)\n- Keeps an onchain feed (`getTips` / `getRecentTips`), tracks `totalTipped`, `jarBalance`, `tipCount`, per-address totals; owner-only `withdraw()`\n- Uses OpenZeppelin `SafeERC20`; custom errors for zero amount / long message / non-owner / empty withdraw\n- **10 Foundry tests pass** (unit + a fuzz test)\n\n**Frontend** (`packages/nextjs`)\n- Home page: hero, connected address, live stats, a two-step **tip form** (adaptive Approve→Send button, presets, balance, message counter) and a **live tip feed**\n- **Connect-wallet** via SE-2's RainbowKit button (burner wallet on local, or MetaMask → localhost:8545)\n- USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts` so scaffold hooks read balance/allowance and call approve\n\n**Local setup (fork mode, per the playbook)**\n- `yarn fork` forks **real Base mainnet** (chain id 31337, `--block-time 1`), so the real USDC contract exists at `0x8335…2913`\n- `yarn deploy` deploys the jar (owner = deployer) and regenerates ABIs\n- `yarn fund-usdc <addr>` helper mints test USDC by impersonating USDC's masterMinter on the fork\n- Frontend targets `chains.foundry` as the skill requires\n\n## Verified\n- `forge test` → 10/10 green\n- Fork live at block 49070298; real USDC symbol/decimals confirmed onchain\n- Full flow over `cast`: approve → tip → feed shows `\"gm from the fork\"`, 10 USDC, balances moved\n- Headless-Chrome render of `http://localhost:3000` shows the feed, form, and Connect button populated **client-side** from the fork; `/` and `/debug` both return 200\n\n## One notable fix\nRainbowKit→wagmi pulls in Coinbase/Base packages that lazily import optional `@x402/*` modules, 500'ing the page. Fixed with a webpack `IgnorePlugin(^@x402/)` in `next.config.ts` — and since Next 16 defaults `next dev` to Turbopack (which ignores that config), the dev/start scripts now run `next dev --webpack`.\n\nNothing was deployed anywhere. Setup steps are documented in `usdc-tip-jar/README.md`. The dev processes (fork, frontend) are still running in the background if you want to click through it; note the fork's chain state (the one test tip, funded balances) resets if you restart `yarn fork`."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":88,"cache_read_input_tokens":136243,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":88},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"02592467-74a3-4595-88ca-bad6f02d1178","uuid":"b45449d7-6134-4a10-b6e9-dfed77dfc6f2","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:04:25.884Z","request_id":"req_011CdMe3XuEZWPUUPLdnT8j1"}
